# Forecasting record

Simulation date for the initial full-market forecast is 2026-06-24. Use the simulation date from the forecasting tools and market snapshot, not the host clock. Evidence must be date capped; articles in a daily folder occasionally have a later publication date and must be excluded until eligible.

All 252 initial questions were assigned for coverage: early_markets rows 0–62, middle_markets rows 63–125, late_middle_markets rows 126–188, root_markets rows 189–251. Each group keeps a dated JSON record in memory. The read-only predictions directory gives submission receipts. market.csv is a snapshot and may not reflect in-session submissions until the next day.

Forecasts use at most five concrete outcomes. For large or unannounced fields, retain genuine unallocated probability instead of normalizing a speculative shortlist to one. A substantive negative result such as None, Draw, No court, or No winning scorer is distinct from an unknown placeholder, and is used when the resolution rules allow it.

Review priorities: newly announced participants and appointments, conditional transfer destinations, World Cup bracket and scoring leaders, F1 development changes, storm tracks shortly before landfall, and legal deadlines. Do not treat old articles about similar past events as current confirmations. Maintain coherence across related markets without erasing event-specific uncertainty.

Initial evidence highlights: Mercedes won six of seven F1 races through Barcelona, Antonelli five; Hamilton second in standings. Alcaraz is injured and absent from Wimbledon. Sinner is strongly favored there. Yu Yiting set 200 IM Asian record 2:06.82; Yu Zidi set 400 IM PB 4:30.79. Hania El Hammamy narrowly leads Amina Orfi in squash rankings. Berlin CDU leads June Civey poll 22 to AfD18/Greens17. Munich, Rhine-Ruhr and Berlin remain German Olympic bidders; Hamburg withdrew. Official Puyo Puyo Japanese entrant is Yuuki Kurihara (ゆうき).


## 2026-06-25 daily review

All 252 questions remain active and covered; no resolutions. Submitted 14 revisions and retained 238. See daily_summary_2026-06-25.json and the four group update files for receipts and reasons. Used 29 searches today.

Corrected prior eligibility and roster omissions: Erriyon Knighton is banned through 2029, Jofra Archer is active in England's XI, and the full FOMC hike-vote set has 12 members. June26 Fed pricing around 25–30% for a July hike repeats the June25 PCE news already incorporated. Avoid counting repeated news as independent evidence.
